{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Consistency metrics for structured information extraction with self-consistency.\\n\\nImplements three complementary consistency measures over N sampled extractions:\\n1. Fleiss' Kappa (surface matching) — treats N samples as N raters on entity presence\\n2. Structural soft Jaccard — span-level soft overlap with Hungarian matching\\n3. Oracle best-of-N — upper-bound F1 by selecting the best sample per instance\\n\\nKey dependencies: numpy, scipy (linear_sum_assignment).\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nfrom itertools import combinations\\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment\\n\\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\\n    pass\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Type aliases (mirrors shared data format)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\nEntity = dict       # {\\\"text\\\", \\\"type\\\", \\\"start\\\", \\\"end\\\"}\\nRelation = dict     # {\\\"head\\\", \\\"tail\\\", \\\"type\\\", ...}\\nEvent = dict        # {\\\"trigger\\\": {...}, \\\"arguments\\\": [...]}\\nExtraction = dict   # {\\\"entities\\\", \\\"relations\\\", \\\"events\\\"}\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# 1. Fleiss' Kappa — surface-level entity agreement\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef _extract_surface_keys(sample: Extraction, subtask: str) -> set[tuple]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract surface-level keys from a sample for Fleiss' Kappa computation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n        return {(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in sample.get(\\\"entities\\\", [])}\\n    elif subtask == \\\"re\\\":\\n        return {(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in sample.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])}\\n    elif subtask == \\\"eae\\\":\\n        keys: set[tuple] = set()\\n        for ev in sample.get(\\\"events\\\", []):\\n            t = ev.get(\\\"trigger\\\", {})\\n            for arg in ev.get(\\\"arguments\\\", []):\\n                keys.add((t.get(\\\"text\\\", \\\"\\\"), t.get(\\\"type\\\", \\\"\\\"), arg[\\\"role\\\"], arg[\\\"text\\\"]))\\n        return keys\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown subtask: {subtask}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef fleiss_kappa_surface(samples: list[Extraction], subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\") -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute Fleiss' kappa treating N LLM samples as N raters on item presence.\\n\\n    Items are subtask-dependent surface keys:\\n    - ner: (entity_text, entity_type)\\n    - re: (head_text, tail_text, relation_type)\\n    - eae: (trigger_text, trigger_type, role, argument_text)\\n\\n    Args:\\n        samples: List of N extraction dicts.\\n        subtask: \\\"ner\\\", \\\"re\\\", or \\\"eae\\\".\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Fleiss' kappa in [-1, 1].  Returns 1.0 for degenerate cases (<=1 unique\\n        item, perfect agreement, or undefined P_e).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n_raters = len(samples)\\n    if n_raters <= 1:\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    # Collect unique keys and per-sample presence sets\\n    entity_sets: list[set[tuple]] = []\\n    all_keys: set[tuple] = set()\\n    for sample in samples:\\n        keys = _extract_surface_keys(sample, subtask)\\n        entity_sets.append(keys)\\n        all_keys |= keys\\n\\n    n_subjec\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}

\\\"\\\"\\\"Compute micro-averaged NER precision, recall, and F1.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        predictions: List of Extraction dicts (one per instance).\\n        golds: List of Extraction dicts (one per {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Consistency metrics for structured information extraction with self-consistency.\\n\\nImplements three complementary consistency measures over N sampled extractions:\\n1. Fleiss' Kappa (surface matching) — treats N samples as N raters on entity presence\\n2. Structural soft Jaccard — span-level soft overlap with Hungarian matching\\n3. Oracle best-of-N — upper-bound F1 by selecting the best sample per instance\\n\\nKey dependencies: numpy, scipy (linear_sum_assignment).\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nfrom itertools import combinations\\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment\\n\\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\\n    pass\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Type aliases (mirrors shared data format)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\nEntity = dict       # {\\\"text\\\", \\\"type\\\", \\\"start\\\", \\\"end\\\"}\\nRelation = dict     # {\\\"head\\\", \\\"tail\\\", \\\"type\\\", ...}\\nEvent = dict        # {\\\"trigger\\\": {...}, \\\"arguments\\\": [...]}\\nExtraction = dict   # {\\\"entities\\\", \\\"relations\\\", \\\"events\\\"}\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# 1. Fleiss' Kappa — surface-level entity agreement\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef _extract_surface_keys(sample: Extraction, subtask: str) -> set[tuple]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract surface-level keys from a sample for Fleiss' Kappa computation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n        return {(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in sample.get(\\\"entities\\\", [])}\\n    elif subtask == \\\"re\\\":\\n        return {(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in sample.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])}\\n    elif subtask == \\\"eae\\\":\\n        keys: set[tuple] = set()\\n        for ev in sample.get(\\\"events\\\", []):\\n            t = ev.get(\\\"trigger\\\", {})\\n            for arg in ev.get(\\\"arguments\\\", []):\\n                keys.add((t.get(\\\"text\\\", \\\"\\\"), t.get(\\\"type\\\", \\\"\\\"), arg[\\\"role\\\"], arg[\\\"text\\\"]))\\n        return keys\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown subtask: {subtask}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef fleiss_kappa_surface(samples: list[Extraction], subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\") -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute Fleiss' kappa treating N LLM samples as N raters on item presence.\\n\\n    Items are subtask-dependent surface keys:\\n    - ner: (entity_text, entity_type)\\n    - re: (head_text, tail_text, relation_type)\\n    - eae: (trigger_text, trigger_type, role, argument_text)\\n\\n    Args:\\n        samples: List of N extraction dicts.\\n        subtask: \\\"ner\\\", \\\"re\\\", or \\\"eae\\\".\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Fleiss' kappa in [-1, 1].  Returns 1.0 for degenerate cases (<=1 unique\\n        item, perfect agreement, or undefined P_e).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n_raters = len(samples)\\n    if n_raters <= 1:\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    # Collect unique keys and per-sample presence sets\\n    entity_sets: list[set[tuple]] = []\\n    all_keys: set[tuple] = set()\\n    for sample in samples:\\n        keys = _extract_surface_keys(sample, subtask)\\n        entity_sets.append(keys)\\n        all_keys |= keys\\n\\n    n_subjects = len(all_keys)\\n    if n_subjects <= 0:\\n        # All samples extracted nothing — perfect agreement on empty set\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    key_list = sorted(all_keys)\\n\\n    # Build rating matrix: n_subjects x 2 (categories: absent=0, present=1)\\n    # n_ij = number of raters assigning subject i to category j\\n    rating = np.zeros((n_subjects, 2), dtype=np.int64)\\n    for es in entity_sets:\\n        for idx, key in enumerate(key_list):\\n            if key in es:\\n                rating[idx, 1] += 1\\n            else:\\n                rating[idx, 0] += 1\\n\\n    n = n_raters\\n    k = 2  # number of categories\\n\\n    # Check perfect agreement: every subject has all votes in one category\\n    if np.all(np.max(rating, axis=1) == n):\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    # P_i for each subject\\n    P_i = (np.sum(rating ** 2, axis=1) - n) / (n * (n - 1))\\n    P_bar = np.mean(P_i)\\n\\n    # P_e — expected agreement by chance\\n    p_j = np.sum(rating, axis=0) / (n_subjects * n)\\n    P_e = np.sum(p_j ** 2)\\n\\n    if abs(1.0 - P_e) < 1e-12:\\n\\n... Oracle best-of-N\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef oracle_best_of_n(\\n    samples: list[Extraction],\\n    gold: Extraction,\\n    subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\",\\n) -> tuple[int, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Select the sample with the highest F1 against the gold extraction.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        samples: N extraction dicts.\\n        gold: Gold-standard extraction.\\n        subtask: Passed through to per_instance_f1.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (best_index, best_f1). Returns (-1, 0.0) if samples is empty.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not samples:\\n        return (-1, 0.0)\\n\\n    # Lazy import to avoid circular dependency with evaluation.py\\n    from evaluation import per_instance_f1\\n\\n    best_idx = -1\\n    best_f1 = -1.0\\n\\n    for idx, sample in enumerate(samples):\\n        f1 = per_instance_f1(sample, gold, subtask=subtask)\\n        if f1 > best_f1:\\n            best_f1 = f1\\n            best_idx = idx\\n\\n    return (best_idx, best_f1)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Aggregate helper\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef compute_all_consistency_scores(\\n    sampled_instances: list[dict],\\n    subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\",\\n) -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute all consistency metrics for a list of sampled instances.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        sampled_instances: List of SampledInstance dicts, each with keys\\n            \\\"id\\\", \\\"text\\\", \\\"gold\\\" (Extraction), \\\"samples\\\" (list[Extraction]).\\n        subtask: \\\"ner\\\", \\\"re\\\", or \\\"eae\\\".\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Dict with per-instance score lists::\\n\\n            {\\n                \\\"fleiss_kappa\\\": [float, ...],\\n                \\\"soft_jaccard\\\": [float, ...],\\n                \\\"oracle_f1\\\":    [float, ...],\\n                \\\"oracle_indices\\\": [int, ...],\\n            }\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    result: dict[str, list] = {\\n        \\\"fleiss_kappa\\\": [],\\n        \\\"soft_jaccard\\\": [],\\n        \\\"oracle_f1\\\": [],\\n        \\\"oracle_indices\\\": [],\\n    }\\n\\n    for inst in sampled_instances:\\n        samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"]\\n        gold = inst[\\\"gold\\\"]\\n\\n        result[\\\"fleiss_kappa\\\"].append(fleiss_kappa_surface(samples, subtask=subtask))\\n        result[\\\"soft_jaccard\\\"].append(\\n            structural_consistency_soft_jaccard(samples, subtask=subtask)\\n        )\\n\\n        best_idx, best_f1 = oracle_best_of_n(samples, gold, subtask=subtask)\\n        result[\\\"oracle_f1\\\"].append(best_f1)\\n        result[\\\"oracle_indices\\\"].append(best_idx)\\n\\n    return result\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.4}}"}

Fleiss' Kappa — surface-level entity agreement\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef _extract_surface_keys(sample: Extraction, subtask: str) -> set[tuple]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract surface-level keys from a sample for Fleiss' Kappa computation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n        return {(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in sample.get(\\\"entities\\\", [])}\\n    elif subtask == \\\"re\\\":\\n        return {(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in sample.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])}\\n    elif subtask == \\\"eae\\\":\\n        keys: set[tuple] = set()\\n        for ev in sample.get(\\\"events\\\", []):\\n            t = ev.get(\\\"trigger\\\", {})\\n            for arg in ev.get(\\\"arguments\\\", []):\\n                keys.add((t.get(\\\"text\\\", \\\"\\\"), t.get(\\\"type\\\", \\\"\\\"), arg[\\\"role\\\"], arg[\\\"text\\\"]))\\n        return keys\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown subtask: {subtask}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef fleiss_kappa_surface(samples: list[Extraction], subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\") -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute Fleiss' kappa treating N LLM samples as N raters on item presence.\\n\\n    Items are subtask-dependent surface keys:\\n    - ner: (entity_text, entity_type)\\n    - re: (head_text, tail_text, relation_type)\\n    - eae: (trigger_text, trigger_type, role, argument_text)\\n\\n    Args:\\n        samples: List of N extraction dicts.\\n        subtask: \\\"ner\\\", \\\"re\\\", or \\\"eae\\\".\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Fleiss' kappa in [-1, 1].  Returns 1.0 for degenerate cases (<=1 unique\\n        item, perfect agreement, or undefined P_e).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n_raters = len(samples)\\n    if n_raters <= 1:\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    # Collect unique keys and per-sample presence sets\\n    entity_sets: list[set[tuple]] = []\\n    all_keys: set[tuple] = set()\\n    for sample in samples:\\n        keys = _extract_surface_keys(sample, subtask)\\n        entity_sets.append(keys)\\n        all_keys |= keys\\n\\n    n_subjects = len(all_keys)\\n    if n_subjects <= 0:\\n        # All samples extracted nothing — perfect agreement on empty set\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    key_list = sorted(all_keys)\\n\\n    # Build rating matrix: n_subjects x 2 (categories: absent=0, present=1)\\n    # n_ij = number of raters assigning subject i to category j\\n    rating = np.zeros((n_subjects, 2), dtype=np.int64)\\n    for es in entity_sets:\\n        for idx, key in enumerate(key_list):\\n            if key in es:\\n                rating[idx, 1] += 1\\n            else:\\n                rating[idx, 0] += 1\\n\\n    n = n_raters\\n    k = 2  # number of categories\\n\\n    # Check perfect agreement: every subject has all votes in one category\\n    if np.all(np.max(rating, axis=1) == n):\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    # P_i for each subject\\n    P_i = (np.sum(rating ** 2, axis=1) - n) / (n * (n - 1))\\n    P_bar = np.mean(P_i)\\n\\n    # P_e — expected agreement by chance\\n    p_j = np.sum(rating, axis=0) / (n_subjects * n)\\n    P_e = np.sum(p_j ** 2)\\n\\n    if abs(1.0 - P_e) < 1e-12:\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 5252 字符] ...\\n\\nn enumerate(ga):\\n            for j, ab in enumerate(gb):\\n                cost[i, j] = _span_soft_jaccard(aa[\\\"start\\\"], aa[\\\"end\\\"],\\n                                                 ab[\\\"start\\\"], ab[\\\"end\\\"])\\n\\n        row_ind, col_ind = linear_sum_assignment(-cost)\\n        matched_sim = cost[row_ind, col_ind].sum()\\n        total_score += matched_sim\\n\\n    if total_weight == 0:\\n        return 1.0\\n    return total_score / total_weight\\n\\n\\ndef structural_consistency_soft_jaccard(\\n    samples: list[Extraction],\\n    subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\",\\n) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Span-level soft Jaccard consistency averaged over all sample pairs.\\n\\n    For each pair (i, j) with i < j, computes a type-aware soft Jaccard using\\n    Hungarian matching on span overlaps, then returns the mean.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        samples: N extraction dicts.\\n        subtask: \\\"ner\\\" for entity consistency, \\\"eae\\\" for event-argument consistency.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Mean pairwise soft Jaccard in [0, 1].\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n = len(samples)\\n    if n <= 1:\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n        pair_fn = _ner_soft_jaccard_pair\\n    elif subtask == \\\"re\\\":\\n        pair_fn = _re_soft_jaccard_pair\\n    else:\\n        pair_fn = _eae_soft_jaccard_pair\\n\\n    field = {\\\"ner\\\": \\\"entities\\\", \\\"re\\\": \\\"relations\\\", \\\"eae\\\": \\\"events\\\"}[subtask]\\n\\n    scores: list[float] = []\\n    for i, j in combinations(range(n), 2):\\n        score = pair_fn(\\n            samples[i].get(field, []),\\n            samples[j].get(field, []),\\n        )\\n        scores.append(score)\\n\\n    return float(np.mean(scores))\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# 3. Oracle best-of-N\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef oracle_best_of_n(\\n    samples: list[Extraction],\\n    gold: Extraction,\\n    subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\",\\n) -> tuple[int, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Select the sample with the highest F1 against the gold extraction.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        samples: N extraction dicts.\\n        gold: Gold-standard extraction.\\n        subtask: Passed through to per_instance_f1.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (best_index, best_f1). Returns (-1, 0.0) if samples is empty.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not samples:\\n        return (-1, 0.0)\\n\\n    # Lazy import to avoid circular dependency with evaluation.py\\n    from evaluation import per_instance_f1\\n\\n    best_idx = -1\\n    best_f1 = -1.0\\n\\n    for idx, sample in enumerate(samples):\\n        f1 = per_instance_f1(sample, gold, subtask=subtask)\\n        if f1 > best_f1:\\n            best_f1 = f1\\n            best_idx = idx\\n\\n    return (best_idx, best_f1)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Aggregate helper\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef compute_all_consistency_scores(\\n    sampled_instances: list[dict],\\n    subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\",\\n) -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute all consistency metrics for a list of sampled instances.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        sampled_instances: List of SampledInstance dicts, each with keys\\n            \\\"id\\\", \\\"text\\\", \\\"gold\\\" (Extraction), \\\"samples\\\" (list[Extraction]).\\n        subtask: \\\"ner\\\", \\\"re\\\", or \\\"eae\\\".\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Dict with per-instance score lists::\\n\\n            {\\n                \\\"fleiss_kappa\\\": [float, ...],\\n                \\\"soft_jaccard\\\": [float, ...],\\n                \\\"oracle_f1\\\":    [float, ...],\\n                \\\"oracle_indices\\\": [int, ...],\\n            }\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    result: dict[str, list] = {\\n        \\\"fleiss_kappa\\\": [],\\n        \\\"soft_jaccard\\\": [],\\n        \\\"oracle_f1\\\": [],\\n        \\\"oracle_indices\\\": [],\\n    }\\n\\n    for inst in sampled_instances:\\n        samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"]\\n        gold = inst[\\\"gold\\\"]\\n\\n        result[\\\"fleiss_kappa\\\"].append(fleiss_kappa_surface(samples, subtask=subtask))\\n        result[\\\"soft_jaccard\\\"].append(\\n            structural_consistency_soft_jaccard(samples, subtask=subtask)\\n        )\\n\\n        best_idx, best_f1 = oracle_best_of_n(samples, gold, subtask=subtask)\\n        result[\\\"oracle_f1\\\"].append(best_f1)\\n {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"def _span_soft_jaccard(s1_start: int, s1_end: int, s2_start: int, s2_end: int) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Soft Jaccard between two character spans.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    overlap = max(0, min(s1_end, s2_end) - max(s1_start, s2_start))\\n    len1 = s1_end - s1_start\\n    len2 = s2_end - s2_start\\n    union = len1 + len2 - overlap\\n    if union <= 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return overlap / union\\n\\n\\ndef _ner_soft_jaccard_pair(entities_a: list[Entity], entities_b: list[Entity]) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute type-aware soft Jaccard between two entity lists via Hungarian matching.\\n\\n    Groups entities by type, finds optimal alignment within each group using\\n    span-level soft Jaccard as the similarity, then computes a weighted average.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        entities_a: Entities from sample i.\\n        entities_b: Entities from sample j.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Weighted-average soft Jaccard across all type groups.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not entities_a and not entities_b:\\n        return 1.0\\n    if not entities_a or not entities_b:\\n        return 0.0\\n\\n    # Group by entity type\\n    types: set[str] = set()\\n    groups_a: dict[str, list[Entity]] = {}\\n    groups_b: dict[str, list[Entity]] = {}\\n\\n    for e in entities_a:\\n        t = e[\\\"type\\\"]\\n        types.add(t)\\n        groups_a.setdefault(t, []).append(e)\\n    for e in entities_b:\\n        t = e[\\\"type\\\"]\\n        types.add(t)\\n        groups_b.setdefault(t, []).append(e)\\n\\n    total_score = 0.0\\n    total_weight = 0\\n\\n    for t in types:\\n        ga = groups_a.get(t, [])\\n        gb = groups_b.get(t, [])\\n        denom = max(len(ga), len(gb))\\n        if denom == 0:\\n            continue\\n\\n        total_weight += denom\\n\\n        if not ga or not gb:\\n            # One side empty — all unmatched, contributes 0\\n            continue\\n\\n        # Cost matrix for Hungarian (we maximize similarity, so negate for min-cost)\\n        cost = np.zeros((len(ga), len(gb)), dtype=np.float64)\\n        for i, ea in enumerate(ga):\\n            for j, eb in enumerate(gb):\\n                cost[i, j] = _span_soft_jaccard(ea[\\\"start\\\"], ea[\\\"end\\\"],\\n                                                 eb[\\\"start\\\"], eb[\\\"end\\\"])\\n\\n        row_ind, col_ind = linear_sum_assignment(-cost)  # 0.0\\n        std = 0.0\\n        pct_f1_zero = 100.0\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"all_sample_f1s\\\": all_sample_f1s,\\n        \\\"mean\\\": mean,\\n        \\\"std\\\": std,\\n        \\\"pct_f1_zero\\\": pct_f1_zero,\\n        \\\"per_instance\\\": per_instance,\\n    }\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Correlation\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef spearman_correlation(\\n    scores_a: list[float],\\n    scores_b: list[float],\\n) -> tuple[float, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute Spearman rank correlation between two score lists.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        scores_a: First list of scores.\\n        scores_b: Second list of scores (same length as scores_a).\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (rho, p_value).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(scores_a) != len(scores_b):\\n        raise ValueError(\\n            f\\\"Score lists must have equal length, got {len(scores_a)} vs {len(scores_b)}\\\"\\n        )\\n    if len(scores_a) < 3:\\n        return (0.0, 1.0)\\n    result = spearmanr(scores_a, scores_b)\\n    return (float(result.statistic), float(result.pvalue))\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Summary report\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef generate_evaluation_report(\\n    sampled_instances: list[dict],\\n    consistency_scores: dict[str, list[float]],\\n    subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\",\\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate a structured evaluation report.\\n\\n    Computes greedy F1, oracle best-of-N F1, and consistency-F1 correlation\\n    for each consistency scoring method.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        sampled_instances: List of SampledInstance dicts, each containing\\n            \\\"id\\\", \\\"text\\\", \\\"gold\\\" (Extraction), \\\"samples\\\" (list of Extraction),\\n            and optionally \\\"greedy\\\" (Extraction).\\n        consistency_scores: Dict mapping method name to a list of per-instance\\n            consistency scores (aligned with sampled_instances).\\n        subtask: \\\"ner\\\" or \\\"eae\\\".\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Dict with keys:\\n            - \\\"greedy_f1\\\": float, micro-averaged F1 of greedy outputs.\\n            - \\\"oracle_best_of_n_f1\\\": float, micro-averaged F1 using the\\n              best sample per instance.\\n            - \\\"per_instance_greedy_f1\\\": list of per-instance greedy F1 values.\\n            - \\\"correlations\\\": dict mapping method name to {\\\"rho\\\": float, \\\"p_value\\\": float}.\\n            - \\\"num_instances\\\": int.\\n            - \\\"num_samples_per_instance\\\": float, average number of samples.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    greedy_preds = []\\n    greedy_golds = []\\n    oracle_preds = []\\n    oracle_golds = []\\n    instance_greedy_f1s: list[float] = []\\n\\n    for inst in sampled_instances:\\n        gold = inst[\\\"gold\\\"]\\n\\n        # Greedy F1\\n        greedy = inst.get(\\\"greedy\\\")\\n        if greedy is not None:\\n            greedy_preds.append(greedy)\\n            greedy_golds.append(gold)\\n            instance_greedy_f1s.append(per_instance_f1(greedy, gold, subtask))\\n        else:\\n            instance_greedy_f1s.append(0.0)\\n\\n        # Oracle best-of-N: pick the sample with highest per-instance F1\\n        samples = inst.get(\\\"samples\\\", [])\\n        if samples:\\n            best_sample = max(samples, key=lambda s: per_instance_f1(s, gold, subtask))\\n            oracle_preds.append(best_sample)\\n            oracle_golds.append(gold)\\n\\n    # Micro-averaged scores\\n    f1_fn = {\\\"ner\\\": compute_ner_f1, \\\"re\\\": compute_re_f1, \\\"eae\\\": compute_eae_f1}[subtask]\\n    greedy_metrics = f1_fn(greedy_preds, greedy_golds) if greedy_preds else _prf(0, 0, 0)\\n    oracle_metrics = f1_fn(oracle_preds, oracle_golds) if oracle_preds else _prf(0, 0, 0)\\n\\n    # Correlation between consistency scores and per-instance greedy F1\\n    correlations: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}\\n    for method_name, scores in consistency_scores.items():\\n        rho, p_value = spearman_correlation(scores, instance_greedy_f1s)\\n        correlations[method_name] = {\\\"rho\\\": rho, \\\"p_value\\\": p_value}\\n\\n    total_samples = sum(len(inst.get(\\\"samples\\\", [])) for inst in sampled_instances)\\n    n = len(sampled_instances)\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"greedy_f1\\\": greedy_metrics[\\\"f1\\\"],\\n        \\\"oracle_best_of_n_f1\\\": oracle_metrics[\\\"f1\\\"],\\n        \\\"per_instance_greedy_f1\\\": instance_greedy_f1s,\\n        \\\"correlations\\\": correlations,\\n        \\\"num_instances\\\": n,\\n        \\\"num_samples_per_instance\\\": total_samples / n if n > 0 else 0.0,\\n    }\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Internal helpers\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef _prf(tp: int, fp: int, fn: int) -> dict[str, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute precision, recall, F1 from raw counts.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0.0\\n    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0.0\\n    return {\\\"precision\\\": precision, \\\"recall\\\": recall, \\\"f1\\\": f1}\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Internal helpers\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef _prf(tp: int, fp: int, fn: int) -> dict[str, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute precision, recall, F1 from raw counts.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0.0\\n    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0.0\\n    return {\\\"precision\\\": precision, \\\"recall\\\": recall, \\\"f1\\\": f1}\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"

Verifying the cost matrices match...

Actually, I can see the logic checks out—when types match, the cost is head_sj × tail_sj × 1, and when they don't, it's 0, so the Hungarian algorithm will produce identical results. Now I'm ready to write the full implementation.

For the hypothesis test decomposition, I'll track each matched pair's head_sj and tail_sj values as individual contributions, then calculate what the overall ρ would be if we used just the head_sj component with the same matching. Type-mismatched pairs contributing zero to head_sj is actually the correct behavior here.

I'm going to add a few more granular metrics to isolate the tail component's effect: head_sj_typed and tail_sj_typed, which only count contributions from type-matched pairs. This way I can directly compare the multiplicative score against its individual components and see exactly how much the tail signal adds beyond the head signal.

Actually, I'm overcomplicating this. Let me stick with the core five metrics plus Fleiss' kappa and see what the data shows—I can always expand later if needed.

Writing the final script...

rels_b:\n        return {k: 1.0 for k in ['head_sj', 'tail_sj', 'type_match', 'multiplicative', 'additive']}\n    if not rels_a or not rels_b:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in ['head_sj', 'tail_sj', 'type_match', 'multiplicative', 'additive']}\n\n    na, nb = len(rels_a), len(rels_b)\n    denom = max(na, nb)\n\n    head_sj_mat = np.zeros((na, nb))\n    tail_sj_mat = np.zeros((na, nb))\n    type_match_mat = np.zeros((na, nb))\n\n    for i, a in enumerate(rels_a):\n        for j, b in enumerate(rels_b):\n            head_sj_mat[i, j] = _span_soft_jaccard(\n                a[\"head_start\"], a[\"head_end\"], b[\"head_start\"], b[\"head_end\"])\n            tail_sj_mat[i, j] = _span_soft_jaccard(\n                a[\"tail_start\"], a[\"tail_end\"], b[\"tail_start\"], b[\"tail_end\"])\n            type_match_mat[i, j] = 1.0 if a[\"type\"] == b[\"type\"] else 0.0\n\n    # Same cost as original _re_soft_jaccard_pair\n    cost = head_sj_mat * tail_sj_mat * type_match_mat\n    row_ind, col_ind = linear_sum_assignment(-cost)\n\n    matched_head = head_sj_mat[row_ind, col_ind].sum()\n    matched_tail = tail_sj_mat[row_ind, col_ind].sum()\n    matched_type = type_match_mat[row_ind, col_ind].sum()\n    matched_mult = cost[row_ind, col_ind].sum()\n\n    additive_mat = (head_sj_mat + tail_sj_mat) / 2 * type_match_mat\n    matched_add = additive_mat[row_ind, col_ind].sum()\n\n    return {\n        'head_sj': matched_head / denom,\n        'tail_sj': matched_tail / denom,\n        'type_match': matched_type / denom,\n        'multiplicative': matched_mult / denom,\n        'additive': matched_add / denom,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_instance_components(samples):\n    n = len(samples)\n    if n <= 1:\n        return {k: 1.0 for k in ['head_sj', 'tail_sj', 'type_match', 'multiplicative', 'additive']}\n\n    pair_scores = {k: [] for k in ['head_sj', 'tail_sj', 'type_match', 'multiplicative', 'additive']}\n    for i, j in combinations(range(n), 2):\n        scores = compute_pair_components(\n            samples[i].get('relations', []),\n            samples[j].get('relations', []))\n        for k, v in scores.items():\n            pair_scores[k].append(v)\n\n    return {k: float(np.mean(v)) for k, v in pair_scores.items()}\n\n\ndef main():\n    data_path = '/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/mvp_pilot_004/samples.jsonl'\n    instances = []\n    with open(data_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            instances.append(json.loads(line))\n\n    re_instances = [inst for inst in instances if inst['gold'].get('relations', [])]\n    n_full = len(re_instances)\n\n    components = {k: [] for k in ['head_sj', 'tail_sj', 'type_match', 'multiplicative', 'additive']}\n    fleiss_scores = []\n    greedy_f1s = []\n    original_sj = []\n\n    for idx, inst in enumerate(re_instances):\n        if idx % 50 == 0:\n            print(f\"  Processing {idx}/{n_full}...\", flush=True)\n\n        comp = compute_instance_components(inst['samples'])\n        for k in components:\n            components[k].append(comp[k])\n\n        fleiss_scores.append(fleiss_kappa_surface(inst['samples'], subtask='re'))\n        original_sj.append(structural_consistency_soft_jaccard(inst['samples'], subtask='re'))\n\n        greedy = inst.get('greedy', inst['samples'][0])\n        greedy_f1s.append(per_instance_f1(greedy, inst['gold'], subtask='re'))\n\n    # Conditional mask: at least one sample has F1 > 0\n    cond_mask = []\n    for inst in re_instances:\n        any_pos = any(per_instance_f1(s, inst['gold'], subtask='re') > 0 for s in inst['samples'])\n        cond_mask.append(any_pos)\n    n_cond = sum(cond_mask)\n\n    print(f\"\\n=== E1: Component-wise RE SJ Decomposition ===\")\n    print(f\"Instances: {n_full} (gold-nonempty RE)\")\n    print(f\"Conditional instances: {n_cond} (filtered all-samples-F1=0)\")\n\n    # Verification\n    mult_rho = stats.spearmanr(components['multiplicative'], greedy_f1s).statistic\n    orig_rho = stats.spearmanr(original_sj, greedy_f1s).statistic\n    mult_vs_orig_diff = np.mean(np.abs(np.array(components['multiplicative']) - np.array(original_sj)))\n    print(f\"\\nVerification:\")\n    print(f\"  multiplicative ρ = {mult_rho:.4f}, original_sj ρ = {orig_rho:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"  mean |multiplicative - original_sj| = {mult_vs_orig_diff:.6f}\")\n\n    all_scores = {**components, 'fleiss_kappa': fleiss_scores}\n\n    def print_table(subset_name, n, score_dict, f1s):\n        print(f\"\\n--- {subset_name} ({n} instances) ---\")\n        print(f\"{'Component':<20} | {'ρ':>8} | {'p-value':>12} | {'mean score':>10}\")\n        print(\"-\" * 60)\n        for name in ['head_sj', 'tail_sj', 'type_match', 'multiplicative', 'additive', 'fleiss_kappa']:\n            scores = score_dict[name]\n            rho, p = stats.spearmanr(scores, f1s)\n            mean = np.mean(scores)\n            print(f\"{name:<20} | {rho:>8.4f} | {p:>12.3e} | {mean:>10.4f}\")\n\n    print_table(\"Full Set\", n_full, all_scores, greedy_f1s)\n\n    cond_scores = {k: [v[i] for i in range(n_full) if cond_mask[i]] for k, v in all_scores.items()}\n    cond_f1s = [greedy_f1s[i] for i in range(n_full) if cond_mask[i]]\n    print_table(\"Conditional Set\", n_cond, cond_scores, cond_f1s)\n\n    # Diagnosis\n    head_rho_full = stats.spearmanr(components['head_sj'], greedy_f1s).statistic\n    mult_rho_full = stats.spearmanr(components['tail_sj'], greedy_f1s).statistic\n    type_rho_full = stats.spearmanr(components['type_match'], greedy_f1s).statistic\n\n    ner_rho = 0.383\n    print(f\"\\n--- Diagnosis if head_rho_full > mult_rho_full + 0.05:\n        print(f\"\\n>>> CONCLUSION: head_sj alone (ρ={head_rho_full:.4f}) significantly outperforms \"\n              f\"multiplicative I need to abandon the metric decomposition approach and explore something fundamentally different.

### Assistant

_source line 88_

E1 完成。**结论：negative — 乘性惩罚假说被否定。**

关键数据：

| Component | Full ρ | Cond ρ |
|---|---|---|
| head_sj | 0.210 | 0.270 |
| tail_sj | 0.201 | 0.259 |
| type_match | 0.201 | 0.252 |
| **multiplicative** | **0.244** | **0.316** |
| additive | 0.243 | 0.306 |
| fleiss_kappa | **0.278** | **0.307** |

验证通过：multiplicative ρ=0.2441 精确匹配 pilot_004，FK ρ=0.2782 精确匹配。

核心发现：
1. **head_sj ρ=0.210 远低于 NER ρ_sj=0.383**（Δ=-0.173），假说被否定
2. **每个 component 单独都比乘性组合差** — 乘性组合实际上是在 *帮忙*（整合三个信号），不是在 *伤害*
3. **additive fix 无效** — additive ρ ≈ multiplicative ρ（差 0.001）
4. RE 的 SJ 问题不是公式问题，是更根本的：关系级别的 span soft Jaccard 本身就缺乏区分力，Fleiss' kappa 的 surface matching 反而更鲁棒

这排除了方案 B（度量分解）。问题根源不在乘法公式，而在 span-level SJ 度量本身对 RE 任务的适用性。
